主题: 南科大健康校园科普讲座-2026年世界高血压日主题宣讲活动Healthy Campus Science Popularization, SUSTech-2026 World Hypertension Day Awareness Lecture
时间: 2026-05-15 18:57:47
正文:
（再次提醒）各位老师、各位同学：大家好！近年来，心血管疾病危险因素对居民健康的影响日益显著，心血管疾病发病率持续上升。《中国心血管健康与疾病报告2024》显示，心血管疾病已成为我国城乡居民的首位死亡原因（农村48.00%，城市45.86%），给个人和社会带来了沉重的经济与健康负担。近期多名知名人士因心脏疾患离世的事件，也再次提醒我们：关注心血管健康、及早预防刻不容缓。2026年5月17日是第22个世界高血压日，今年的主题为 “共同控制高血压：定期检测血压，战胜无声杀手” 。高血压是心脑血管疾病最重要的可控危险因素，提高对高血压的认知与防控意识，倡导健康生活方式，是守护自身与家人健康的关键。届时，南科大医学院医院管理中心将举办世界高血压日科普知识宣讲活动，邀请中国医学科学院阜外医院深圳医院专家来校开展科普讲座。具体安排如下：**宣讲时间： 2026年5月17日（星期日）10:00 – 11:00**宣讲地点： 南科大专家公寓二栋教职工活动中心**线上参与： 腾讯会议号#327-238-842#**活动内容：1.专家讲座：高血压基础知识与精准测量、防控策略与健康管理、药物治疗原则与误区、自我监测与长期管理技巧；2.线上线下互动问答；3.发起健康倡议，线下发放健康宣传手册等。欢迎各位师生及家属安排时间参加听讲，积极参与互动交流。让我们共同学习高血压防控知识，远离“无声杀手”，守护心脏健康！顺祝 健康平安！南科大医学院医院管理中心2026年5月11日Dear Teachers and Students,In recent years, the impact of cardiovascular disease risk factors on public health has become increasingly significant, and the incidence of cardiovascular disease continues to rise. According to the China Cardiovascular Health and Disease Report 2024, cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death among urban and rural residents in China (48.00% in rural areas and 45.86% in urban areas). The economic burden of cardiovascular disease on individuals and society is increasing, making it a major public health issue. Recent cases of well-known individuals passing away from heart disease have repeatedly reminded us of the urgent need for early attention to and prevention of cardiovascular disease.May 17, 2026 marks the 22nd World Hypertension Day, with this year’s theme: “Control Hypertension Together: Measure Your Blood Pressure Regularly to Defeat the Silent Killer.” To improve the awareness and prevention of hypertension among our faculty and students, and to promote healthy lifestyles, the School of Medicine Hospitals Coordination Center of SUSTech is organizing a special hypertension awareness lecture. We have invited an expert from Fuwai Hospital, Chinese Academy of Medical Sciences, Shenzhen to give the talk.Lecture Time: May 17, 2026 (Sunday), 10:00 – 11:00Location: Faculty Activity Center, Building 2, SUSTech Expert ApartmentOnline participation: Tencent Meeting ID: 327-238-842Lecture Content:1. Expert lecture covering basic knowledge of hypertension, accurate measurement, prevention strategies, health management, principles of medication, common misconceptions, self-monitoring, and long-term management techniques.2. Interactive Q&amp;A session (online and on-site).3. Health initiative and distribution of health brochures (on-site).All faculty, students, and their family members are welcome to attend and participate in the discussion.Wishing you good health!School of Medicine Hospitals Coordination Center, SUSTechMay 11, 2026

发件人: 学生心理成长中心  <counseling@sustech.edu.cn>
主题: 我与食物的关系――与“吃饭焦虑”和解 单次朋辈心理团队辅导 | My Relationship with Food �C Reconciling with "Eating Anxiety" Single-Session Peer Psychological Group Counseling
时间: 2026-05-18 18:00:00
正文:
body { line-height: 1.5; }body { font-size: 21px; font-family: 仿宋; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.5; } 我与食物的关系――与“吃饭焦虑”和解|单次朋辈心理团队辅导&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;吃饭，这个每天都会做的最简单的事情，对于一些人似乎并不简单。面对食物，你是否对它又爱又恨，内心的困扰总是得不到答案：一次次撑得难受后悔发誓再也不吃撑了，撑的想吐却像魔怔了一样一次次重演？路过琳琅满目的面包店，只敢试吃一小块，明明很想买但一直有个声音说“我不配”？手机上下载了卡路里计算器，每顿饭都吃得小心翼翼却在放纵日敞开暴食，但心里却升起一股悲哀？想用食物填补内心那一块，但好像发现越填越虚，身体好像也要垮了？刚吃完早饭就在想午饭吃什么，怎么总有食物噪音干扰正常生活工作？没打到最喜欢的菜就算吃饱了也不满足，要再买个甜品弥补一下……等等，为什么好像只有自己的头顶有永远也填不满的【吃饭】任务条？到底是我吃饭还是饭操纵我？我该怎么办？别担心，【我与食物的关系】团体会提供一个放松、安全的空间，你在这里可以认识类似困扰的伙伴，可以探索问题的答案：在上半场的团体游戏互动里，你可以倾诉自己的困扰，也可以倾听他人的困境：你可能会发现还有很多人头上也有名为【吃饭】的打怪血条；你可以与伙伴探讨与食物关系紧张背后的种种原因……在下半场的团体中我们设置了一次正念进食体验，由心理学社请客（食堂配餐），让我们一起用正念进食的方法吃这顿最平常的一餐，尝试体会和找回那种与食物的初见感和中立关系，相信你会有不一样的发现。团体基本信息（一）团体设置形式：6-12人封闭式心理团体时间：2026年5月24日周日，下午16:30-18:50（上下场各60分钟，间隔20分钟休息与取餐，共140分钟）地点：学生宿舍11栋204室（二）招募对象1.&nbsp;在校本、研同学，有进食相关的困扰，希望改善自己与食物的关系；2.&nbsp;在团体中愿意对自我和他人的经验保持开放，愿意分享和深入探索，通过团体分享和互动共同促进思考、体验和成长的；3.&nbsp;时间预留，可确保到场参与。（三）报名方式1.&nbsp;扫描下方二维码填写报名表填写问卷后工作人员会通过邮件与你确认报名成功与否，并告知具体的参加方式和注意事项，务必记得留意邮箱入选通知哦~2.&nbsp;报名截止时间：5月22日19点整3.&nbsp;为保证团体体验，人数限12人以内，先报先得（四）朋辈带领者简介阎沁琳南科大朋辈心理辅导团队成员南科大环境科学与工程学院2022级博士生、原南科大2018级本科生学生心理成长中心2026年5月15日My&nbsp;Relationship&nbsp;with&nbsp;Food&nbsp;�C&nbsp;Reconciling&nbsp;with&nbsp;"Eating&nbsp;Anxiety"|Single-Session&nbsp;Peer&nbsp;Psychological&nbsp;Group&nbsp;CounselingEating&nbsp;�C&nbsp;the&nbsp;simplest&nbsp;thing&nbsp;we&nbsp;do&nbsp;every&nbsp;day&nbsp;�C&nbsp;is&nbsp;not&nbsp;so&nbsp;simple&nbsp;for&nbsp;some&nbsp;people.When&nbsp;facing&nbsp;food,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;both&nbsp;love&nbsp;and&nbsp;hate,&nbsp;with&nbsp;inner&nbsp;struggles&nbsp;that&nbsp;never&nbsp;seem&nbsp;to&nbsp;find&nbsp;answers. Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;repeatedly&nbsp;overeat&nbsp;until&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;miserable&nbsp;and&nbsp;swear&nbsp;you'll&nbsp;never&nbsp;do&nbsp;it&nbsp;again,&nbsp;yet&nbsp;find&nbsp;yourself&nbsp;trapped&nbsp;in&nbsp;the&nbsp;same&nbsp;compulsive&nbsp;cycle?When&nbsp;passing&nbsp;by&nbsp;a&nbsp;bakery&nbsp;full&nbsp;of&nbsp;tempting&nbsp;treats,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;only&nbsp;dare&nbsp;to&nbsp;try&nbsp;a&nbsp;tiny&nbsp;sample,&nbsp;wanting&nbsp;to&nbsp;buy&nbsp;something&nbsp;but&nbsp;hearing&nbsp;a&nbsp;voice&nbsp;inside&nbsp;say,&nbsp;"I&nbsp;don't&nbsp;deserve&nbsp;it"?Have&nbsp;you&nbsp;downloaded&nbsp;a&nbsp;calorie&nbsp;counter&nbsp;on&nbsp;your&nbsp;phone,&nbsp;eating&nbsp;every&nbsp;meal&nbsp;with&nbsp;extreme&nbsp;caution,&nbsp;only&nbsp;to&nbsp;binge&nbsp;on&nbsp;cheat&nbsp;days&nbsp;while&nbsp;a&nbsp;wave&nbsp;of&nbsp;sadness&nbsp;rises&nbsp;within&nbsp;you?Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;try&nbsp;to&nbsp;fill&nbsp;an&nbsp;inner&nbsp;void&nbsp;with&nbsp;food,&nbsp;only&nbsp;to&nbsp;find&nbsp;that&nbsp;the&nbsp;more&nbsp;you&nbsp;eat,&nbsp;the&nbsp;emptier&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;�C&nbsp;and&nbsp;your&nbsp;body&nbsp;seems&nbsp;to&nbsp;be&nbsp;breaking&nbsp;down&nbsp;too?Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;start&nbsp;thinking&nbsp;about&nbsp;lunch&nbsp;right&nbsp;after&nbsp;finishing&nbsp;breakfast,&nbsp;constantly&nbsp;disturbed&nbsp;by&nbsp;"food&nbsp;noise"&nbsp;that&nbsp;interferes&nbsp;with&nbsp;your&nbsp;daily&nbsp;life&nbsp;and&nbsp;work?If&nbsp;you&nbsp;don't&nbsp;get&nbsp;your&nbsp;favorite&nbsp;dish&nbsp;at&nbsp;the&nbsp;cafeteria,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;unsatisfied&nbsp;even&nbsp;after&nbsp;being&nbsp;full,&nbsp;and&nbsp;need&nbsp;to&nbsp;buy&nbsp;a&nbsp;dessert&nbsp;to&nbsp;compensate?&nbsp;And&nbsp;why&nbsp;does&nbsp;it&nbsp;seem&nbsp;like&nbsp;only&nbsp;you&nbsp;have&nbsp;an&nbsp;endless&nbsp;"eating"&nbsp;quest&nbsp;hanging&nbsp;over&nbsp;your&nbsp;head?&nbsp;Does&nbsp;food&nbsp;control&nbsp;me,&nbsp;or&nbsp;do&nbsp;I&nbsp;control&nbsp;food?&nbsp;What&nbsp;should&nbsp;I&nbsp;do?Don't&nbsp;worry.&nbsp;The&nbsp;"My&nbsp;Relationship&nbsp;with&nbsp;Food"&nbsp;group&nbsp;provides&nbsp;a&nbsp;relaxing,&nbsp;safe&nbsp;space&nbsp;where&nbsp;you&nbsp;can&nbsp;meet&nbsp;peers&nbsp;with&nbsp;similar&nbsp;struggles&nbsp;and&nbsp;explore&nbsp;answers&nbsp;to&nbsp;these&nbsp;questions. 主题: 【温馨提示】关于加强夏季用电安全的温馨提示 Tips on Electrical Safety in Summer
时间: 2026-05-20 14:00:45
正文:
关于加强夏季用电安全的温馨提示&nbsp;各位老师、同学：天气逐渐炎热，学校已进入用电高峰期。为进一步加强学校用电安全管理，切实消除用电安全隐患，杜绝火灾等各类用电安全事故发生，确保学校师生员工生命和财产安全，现就有关事项通知如下：1.夏季使用空调应关好门窗；如遇假期或下班室内无人时，及时关闭空调、电脑、灯具等用电设备，做到“人走电关”，避免不必要的能耗。2.禁止私自拉、接电线，禁止改动供电线路及用电设施设备。避免多台设备共用一个插排，或插排串接使用造成线路过载运行。宿舍内严禁存放和使用大功率电器。3.经常检查设备插头、接线端、线路和开关是否出现发热、绝缘老化现象，发现问题及时报修或更换；漏电保护开关应每月进行一次测试，保障功能完好。4.用电设备应保持干燥，避开空调出风口、水管等易产生冷凝水位置；洁净间电箱开关应做好防水防凝露措施。设备摆放避免阳光直射，四周留出散热空间。5.每月对24小时运转的电气设备（如冰箱、精密空调、打印机、饮水机、空气净化器、洗衣机等）、用电线路进行巡查、停电检修。及时清理供电设施、电气设备、用电线路周边的各类易燃可燃杂物，确保供电设施、用电设备、线路安全。6.电动自行车及电池严禁进楼停放或充电，严禁私自改装，定期维护；手机、充电宝、笔记本等避免长时间充电，充电时远离可燃物。电气设备使用过程中如遇打火、异味、高热、异响等异常情况时，请立即停止操作并关闭电源，及时拨打物业客服电话88010123；如遇突发紧急事件，请及时拨打学校应急电话：88010110、18566211101。&nbsp;保卫部总务后勤部2026年5月20日&nbsp;Tips on Electrical Safety in Summer&nbsp;May 20, 2026&nbsp;Dear all,&nbsp;As&nbsp;it’s getting hot, we have entered the peak season of electricity consumption. To further enhance the electrical safety management, effectively eliminate electrical hazards, fires and other electrical accidents, and ensure the safety of SUSTech members, we have the following tips: &nbsp;1. Close doors and windows when using air conditioners. Turn off the electricity equipment, including air conditioners, PCs and lights, during holidays or after the end of the day when there is no one indoors to avoid unnecessary energy consumption. Do not add or connect wires without permission. Do not change power supply lines and electrical facilities and equipment without permission. Avoid multiple devices sharing a plug, or a plug connecting with another resulting in electrical circuit overload. It is also strictly forbidden to store and use high-wattage appliances in the dormitory.<span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';f...(truncated)

主题: 本科生2026年春季学期期末考试安排的通知   Notice Regarding Final Examination Arrangements for Undergraduate Students for Spring 2026
时间: 2026-05-21 09:53:23
正文:
body { line-height: 1.5; }p { margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; }body { font-size: 21px; font-family: 仿宋; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.5; } 本科生2026年春季学期期末考试安排的通知各位同学：&nbsp; &nbsp; 大家好！&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;2026年春季学期期末考试时间为2026年6月6日—2026年6月18日。现将本学期期末考试安排通知如下：&nbsp;1.不参加期末考试周考试的课程，由任课教师在期末考试周前自行安排，请同学们以任课教师通知为准。&nbsp;2.参加期末考试周考试的课程，请同学们登录教务系统查看考试安排，考试时间、考试地点、考试时长等信息请务必以教务系统为准。考试时长一般为2小时，部分课程考试时长3小时，请留意考试时间。&nbsp;3.考生须携带学生证或校园卡提前10分钟进入考场，并在考试过程中严格遵守考试纪律。开考30分钟后考生不得进入考场，开考30分钟内考生不得离开考场。考试作弊者，按相应规定予以处理。 &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;4.申请缓考的同学，须于考试前填写《南方科技大学本科生期末考试缓考申请表》（附件1），并按照缓考须知的要求附上符合规定的有效证明，按照缓考流程在开考前交至教学工作部（南科大中心304）。已参加考试课程不能申请缓考，毕业学期课程不能申请缓考。由于缓考成绩获得时间较晚，会对选课、选专业、毕业等造成影响，请同学们结合自身情况慎重申请缓考。每门课程只有一次缓考机会，通过缓考申请的同学须在下一学期开学初参加缓考考试，如未参加缓考考试，此门课程按照“0分、旷考”记录成绩单。&nbsp;5.如学生未申请缓考或缓考申请未通过，擅自不参加考试者，按照“缺考0分”记录。&nbsp;6.研究生课程的缓考规则与本科生不同，原则上研究生课程缓考考试在该课程的下一个开课学期与期末考试同时进行，请申请研究生课程缓考的学生根据自身情况慎重选择。&nbsp; &nbsp; 7.请同学们严格遵守考试纪律，相关考试规定请参照《南方科技大学考试工作及成绩管理条例（2017年修订）》（附件2）和《南方科技大学学生处分实施办法》（附件3）。&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; 祝大家考试顺利！如有问题，可回复本邮箱咨询。&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;备注：本学期缓考申请表有更新，如有缓考需求的同学请下载附件中的表格。&nbsp;附件1.&nbsp;南方科技大学本科生期末考试缓考申请表及缓考须知&nbsp;（2026年春季学期更新）&nbsp;附件2. 南方科技大学考试工作及成绩管理条例（2017年修订）&nbsp;附件3.&nbsp;南方科技大学学生处分实施办法&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Notice Regarding Final Examination Arrangements for Undergraduate Students&nbsp;for&nbsp;Spring&nbsp;2026&nbsp;<p class="MsoNormal" style="font-family: &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, Tahoma; margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;...(truncated)

According to the China Cardiovascular Health and Disease Report 2024, cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death among urban and rural residents in China (48.00% in rural areas and 45.86% in urban areas). The economic burden of cardiovascular disease on individuals and society is increasing, making it a major public health issue. Recent cases of well-known individuals passing away from heart disease have repeatedly reminded us of the urgent need for early attention to and prevention of cardiovascular disease.May 17, 2026 marks the 22nd World Hypertension Day, with this year’s theme: “Control Hypertension Together: Measure Your Blood Pressure Regularly to Defeat the Silent Killer.” To improve the awareness and prevention of hypertension among our faculty and students, and to promote healthy lifestyles, the School of Medicine Hospitals Coordination Center of SUSTech is organizing a special hypertension awareness lecture. We have invited an expert from Fuwai Hospital, Chinese Academy of Medical Sciences, Shenzhen to give the talk.Lecture Time: May 17, 2026 (Sunday), 10:00 – 11:00Location: Faculty Activity Center, Building 2, SUSTech Expert ApartmentOnline participation: Tencent Meeting ID: 327-238-842Lecture Content:1. Expert lecture covering basic knowledge of hypertension, accurate measurement, prevention strategies, health management, principles of medication, common misconceptions, self-monitoring, and long-term management techniques.2. Interactive Q&amp;A session (online and on-site).3. Health initiative and distribution of health brochures (on-site).All faculty, students, and their family members are welcome to attend and participate in the discussion.Wishing you good health!School of Medicine Hospitals Coordination Center, SUSTechMay 11, 2026",

The online reading session (from 25 April to 20 May) is now drawing to a close, and an in-person program will be held on 20 May. We warmly invite you to join us in exploring the poetic life of this literary giant of the Northern Song Dynasty and to experience his magnanimity and resilience in the face of adversity.&nbsp;Leading Readers and Topics:&nbsp;Yang Zaiyong: Interprets Su Dongpo’s life struggles and self-transcendence through the lens of philosophy and psychological growth.&nbsp;Liu Qing: Focuses on Su Dongpo’s literary achievements and linguistic expression, interpreting the philosophical reflections on life found in his poetry and prose&nbsp;Wang Wei: Guides us in understanding Su Dongpo’s magnanimous approach to life through the lens of emotional support and spiritual healing.&nbsp;This is a precious opportunity to draw closer to Su Dongpo and gain self-insight through words and dialogue.       "body": "body { line-height: 1.5; }body { font-size: 21px; font-family: 仿宋; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.5; } 我与食物的关系――与“吃饭焦虑”和解|单次朋辈心理团队辅导&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;吃饭，这个每天都会做的最简单的事情，对于一些人似乎并不简单。面对食物，你是否对它又爱又恨，内心的困扰总是得不到答案：一次次撑得难受后悔发誓再也不吃撑了，撑的想吐却像魔怔了一样一次次重演？路过琳琅满目的面包店，只敢试吃一小块，明明很想买但一直有个声音说“我不配”？手机上下载了卡路里计算器，每顿饭都吃得小心翼翼却在放纵日敞开暴食，但心里却升起一股悲哀？想用食物填补内心那一块，但好像发现越填越虚，身体好像也要垮了？刚吃完早饭就在想午饭吃什么，怎么总有食物噪音干扰正常生活工作？没打到最喜欢的菜就算吃饱了也不满足，要再买个甜品弥补一下……等等，为什么好像只有自己的头顶有永远也填不满的【吃饭】任务条？到底是我吃饭还是饭操纵我？我该怎么办？别担心，【我与食物的关系】团体会提供一个放松、安全的空间，你在这里可以认识类似困扰的伙伴，可以探索问题的答案：在上半场的团体游戏互动里，你可以倾诉自己的困扰，也可以倾听他人的困境：你可能会发现还有很多人头上也有名为【吃饭】的打怪血条；你可以与伙伴探讨与食物关系紧张背后的种种原因……在下半场的团体中我们设置了一次正念进食体验，由心理学社请客（食堂配餐），让我们一起用正念进食的方法吃这顿最平常的一餐，尝试体会和找回那种与食物的初见感和中立关系，相信你会有不一样的发现。团体基本信息（一）团体设置形式：6-12人封闭式心理团体时间：2026年5月24日周日，下午16:30-18:50（上下场各60分钟，间隔20分钟休息与取餐，共140分钟）地点：学生宿舍11栋204室（二）招募对象1.&nbsp;在校本、研同学，有进食相关的困扰，希望改善自己与食物的关系；2.&nbsp;在团体中愿意对自我和他人的经验保持开放，愿意分享和深入探索，通过团体分享和互动共同促进思考、体验和成长的；3.&nbsp;时间预留，可确保到场参与。（三）报名方式1.&nbsp;扫描下方二维码填写报名表填写问卷后工作人员会通过邮件与你确认报名成功与否，并告知具体的参加方式和注意事项，务必记得留意邮箱入选通知哦~2.&nbsp;报名截止时间：5月22日19点整3.&nbsp;为保证团体体验，人数限12人以内，先报先得（四）朋辈带领者简介阎沁琳南科大朋辈心理辅导团队成员南科大环境科学与工程学院2022级博士生、原南科大2018级本科生学生心理成长中心2026年5月15日My&nbsp;Relationship&nbsp;with&nbsp;Food&nbsp;�C&nbsp;Reconciling&nbsp;with&nbsp;\"Eating&nbsp;Anxiety\"|Single-Session&nbsp;Peer&nbsp;Psychological&nbsp;Group&nbsp;CounselingEating&nbsp;�C&nbsp;the&nbsp;simplest&nbsp;thing&nbsp;we&nbsp;do&nbsp;every&nbsp;day&nbsp;�C&nbsp;is&nbsp;not&nbsp;so&nbsp;simple&nbsp;for&nbsp;some&nbsp;people.When&nbsp;facing&nbsp;food,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;both&nbsp;love&nbsp;and&nbsp;hate,&nbsp;with&nbsp;inner&nbsp;struggles&nbsp;that&nbsp;never&nbsp;seem&nbsp;to&nbsp;find&nbsp;answers. Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;repeatedly&nbsp;overeat&nbsp;until&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;miserable&nbsp;and&nbsp;swear&nbsp;you'll&nbsp;never&nbsp;do&nbsp;it&nbsp;again,&nbsp;yet&nbsp;find&nbsp;yourself&nbsp;trapped&nbsp;in&nbsp;the&nbsp;same&nbsp;compulsive&nbsp;cycle?When&nbsp;passing&nbsp;by&nbsp;a&nbsp;bakery&nbsp;full&nbsp;of&nbsp;tempting&nbsp;treats,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;only&nbsp;dare&nbsp;to&nbsp;try&nbsp;a&nbsp;tiny&nbsp;sample,&nbsp;wanting&nbsp;to&nbsp;buy&nbsp;something&nbsp;but&nbsp;hearing&nbsp;a&nbsp;voice&nbsp;inside&nbsp;say,&nbsp;\"I&nbsp;don't&nbsp;deserve&nbsp;it\"?Have&nbsp;you&nbsp;downloaded&nbsp;a&nbsp;calorie&nbsp;counter&nbsp;on&nbsp;your&nbsp;phone,&nbsp;eating&nbsp;every&nbsp;meal&nbsp;with&nbsp;extreme&nbsp;caution,&nbsp;only&nbsp;to&nbsp;binge&nbsp;on&nbsp;cheat&nbsp;days&nbsp;while&nbsp;a&nbsp;wave&nbsp;of&nbsp;sadness&nbsp;rises&nbsp;within&nbsp;you?Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;try&nbsp;to&nbsp;fill&nbsp;an&nbsp;inner&nbsp;void&nbsp;with&nbsp;food,&nbsp;only&nbsp;to&nbsp;find&nbsp;that&nbsp;the&nbsp;more&nbsp;you&nbsp;eat,&nbsp;the&nbsp;emptier&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;�C&nbsp;and&nbsp;your&nbsp;body&nbsp;seems&nbsp;to&nbsp;be&nbsp;breaking&nbsp;down&nbsp;too?Do&nbsp;you&nbsp;start&nbsp;thinking&nbsp;about&nbsp;lunch&nbsp;right&nbsp;after&nbsp;finishing&nbsp;breakfast,&nbsp;constantly&nbsp;disturbed&nbsp;by&nbsp;\"food&nbsp;noise\"&nbsp;that&nbsp;interferes&nbsp;with&nbsp;your&nbsp;daily&nbsp;life&nbsp;and&nbsp;work?If&nbsp;you&nbsp;don't&nbsp;get&nbsp;your&nbsp;favorite&nbsp;dish&nbsp;at&nbsp;the&nbsp;cafeteria,&nbsp;do&nbsp;you&nbsp;feel&nbsp;unsatisfied&nbsp;even&nbsp;after&nbsp;being&nbsp;full,&nbsp;and&nbsp;need&nbsp;to&nbsp;buy&nbsp;a&nbsp;dessert&nbsp;to&nbsp;compensate?&nbsp;And&nbsp;why&nbsp;does&nbsp;it&nbsp;seem&nbsp;like&nbsp;only&nbsp;you&nbsp;have&nbsp;an&nbsp;endless&nbsp;\"eating\"&nbsp;quest&nbsp;hanging&nbsp;over&nbsp;your&nbsp;head?&nbsp;Does&nbsp;food&nbsp;control&nbsp;me,&nbsp;or&nbsp;do&nbsp;I&nbsp;control&nbsp;food?&nbsp;What&nbsp;shou lang=\"EN-US\">5</span>月<span lang=\"EN-US\">23</span>日（周六）<span lang=\"EN-US\">\n14:00-19:00 </span>（含考核），在智华教学楼（原第三教学楼）<span lang=\"EN-US\">107</span>举行，欢迎各位老师、同学报名参加。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span lang=\"EN-US\" style=\"font-size:11.0pt;line-height:150%;\nfont-family:宋体;mso-bidi-font-family:宋体;color:#333333;mso-font-kerning:0pt\">扫描附件中的二维码加入本期培训总群，按照群公告和群文件中的指南完成报名，达到8<span lang=\"EN-US\">0</span>人限额关闭报名通道。请珍惜培训机会，合理安排时间，确保全程培训。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span lang=\"EN-US\" style=\"font-size:11.0pt;\nline-height:150%;font-family:宋体;mso-bidi-font-family:宋体;color:#333333;\nmso-font-kerning:0pt\"><o:p>&nbsp;</o:p></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span lang=\"EN-US\">23</span>日<span lang=\"EN-US\"> 14:00-19:00</span>（含考核）。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span style=\"font-size:11.0pt;line-height:150%;\nfont-family:宋体;mso-bidi-font-family:宋体;color:#333333;mso-font-kerning:0pt\">（二）培训地点：智华教学楼（原第三教学楼）<span lang=\"EN-US\">107</span>。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span style=\"font-size:11.0pt;line-height:150%;\nfont-family:宋体;mso-bidi-font-family:宋体;color:#333333;mso-font-kerning:0pt\">（三）培训对象：面向学校师生、家属、第三方。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span lang=\"EN-US\">https://www.crcntc.org.cn/</span>，完成救护员课程培训与考核。（详见培训总群内群文件）<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\" style=\"text-align:left;line-height:150%;\nmso-pagination:widow-orphan\"><span </span>由南科大商学院王宇教授团队研发，旨在搭建患者与救助者间的信息传递平台，可在院外心脏骤停场景下智能匹配附近急救员，助力在黄金四分钟内为患者提供心肺复苏支持，为校园应急救护网络提供数字化补充。<span lang=\"EN-US\"><o:p></o:p></span></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"left\"       "subject": "【温馨提示】关于加强夏季用电安全的温馨提示 Tips on Electrical Safety in Summer",
      "sender": "\" 保卫部(oss) \" <oss@sustech.edu.cn>",
      "time": "2026-05-20 14:00:45",
      "body": "关于加强夏季用电安全的温馨提示&nbsp;各位老师、同学：天气逐渐炎热，学校已进入用电高峰期。为进一步加强学校用电安全管理，切实消除用电安全隐患，杜绝火灾等各类用电安全事故发生，确保学校师生员工生命和财产安全，现就有关事项通知如下：1.夏季使用空调应关好门窗；如遇假期或下班室内无人时，及时关闭空调、电脑、灯具等用电设备，做到“人走电关”，避免不必要的能耗。2.禁止私自拉、接电线，禁止改动供电线路及用电设施设备。避免多台设备共用一个插排，或插排串接使用造成线路过载运行。宿舍内严禁存放和使用大功率电器。3.经常检查设备插头、接线端、线路和开关是否出现发热、绝缘老化现象，发现问题及时报修或更换；漏电保护开关应每月进行一次测试，保障功能完好。4.用电设备应保持干燥，避开空调出风口、水管等易产生冷凝水位置；洁净间电箱开关应做好防水防凝露措施。设备摆放避免阳光直射，四周留出散热空间。5.每月对24小时运转的电气设备（如冰箱、精密空调、打印机、饮水机、空气净化器、洗衣机等）、用电线路进行巡查、停电检修。及时清理供电设施、电气设备、用电线路周边的各类易燃可燃杂物，确保供电设施、用电设备、线路安全。6.电动自行车及电池严禁进楼停放或充电，严禁私自改装，定期维护；手机、充电宝、笔记本等避免长时间充电，充电时远离可燃物。电气设备使用过程中如遇打火、异味、高热、异响等异常情况时，请立即停止操作并关闭电源，及时拨打物业客服电话88010123；如遇突发紧急事件，请及时拨打学校应急电话：88010110、18566211101。&nbsp;保卫部总务后勤部2026年5月20日&nbsp;Tips on Electrical Safety in Summer&nbsp;May 20, 2026&nbsp;Dear all,&nbsp;As&nbsp;it’s getting hot, we have entered the peak season of electricity consumption. To further enhance the electrical safety management, effectively eliminate electrical hazards, fires and other electrical accidents, and ensure the safety of SUSTech members, we have the following tips: &nbsp;1. Close doors and windows when using air conditioners. Turn off the electricity equipment, including air conditioners, PCs and lights, during holidays or after the end of the day when there is no one indoors to avoid unnecessary energy consumption. Do not add or connect wires without permission. Do not change power supply lines and electrical facilities and equipment without permission. Avoid multiple devices sharing a plug, or a plug connecting with another resulting in electrical circuit overload. It is also strictly forbidden to store and use high-wattage appliances in the dormitory.<span style=\"mso-spacerun:'yes';f...(truncated)",

      "body": "body { line-height: 1.5; }p { margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; }body { font-size: 21px; font-family: 仿宋; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.5; } 本科生2026年春季学期期末考试安排的通知各位同学：&nbsp; &nbsp; 大家好！&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;2026年春季学期期末考试时间为2026年6月6日—2026年6月18日。现将本学期期末考试安排通知如下：&nbsp;1.不参加期末考试周考试的课程，由任课教师在期末考试周前自行安排，请同学们以任课教师通知为准。&nbsp;2.参加期末考试周考试的课程，请同学们登录教务系统查看考试安排，考试时间、考试地点、考试时长等信息请务必以教务系统为准。考试时长一般为2小时，部分课程考试时长3小时，请留意考试时间。&nbsp;3.考生须携带学生证或校园卡提前10分钟进入考场，并在考试过程中严格遵守考试纪律。开考30分钟后考生不得进入考场，开考30分钟内考生不得离开考场。考试作弊者，按相应规定予以处理。 &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;4.申请缓考的同学，须于考试前填写《南方科技大学本科生期末考试缓考申请表》（附件1），并按照缓考须知的要求附上符合规定的有效证明，按照缓考流程在开考前交至教学工作部（南科大中心304）。已参加考试课程不能申请缓考，毕业学期课程不能申请缓考。由于缓考成绩获得时间较晚，会对选课、选专业、毕业等造成影响，请同学们结合自身情况慎重申请缓考。每门课程只有一次缓考机会，通过缓考申请的同学须在下一学期开学初参加缓考考试，如未参加缓考考试，此门课程按照“0分、旷考”记录成绩单。&nbsp;5.如学生未申请缓考或缓考申请未通过，擅自不参加考试者，按照“缺考0分”记录。&nbsp;6.研究生课程的缓考规则与本科生不同，原则上研究生课程缓考考试在该课程的下一个开课学期与期末考试同时进行，请申请研究生课程缓考的学生根据自身情况慎重选择。&nbsp; &nbsp; 7.请同学们严格遵守考试纪律，相关考试规定请参照《南方科技大学考试工作及成绩管理条例（2017年修订）》（附件2）和《南方科技大学学生处分实施办法》（附件3）。&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; 祝大家考试顺利！如有问题，可回复本邮箱咨询。&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;备注：本学期缓考申请表有更新，如有缓考需求的同学请下载附件中的表格。&nbsp;附件1.&nbsp;南方科技大学本科生期末考试缓考申请表及缓考须知&nbsp;（2026年春季学期更新）&nbsp;附件2. 南方科技大学考试工作及成绩管理条例（2017年修订）&nbsp;附件3.&nbsp;南方科技大学学生处分实施办法&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Notice Regarding Final Examination Arrangements for Undergraduate Students&nbsp;for&nbsp;Spring&nbsp;2026&nbsp;<p class=\"MsoNormal\" style=\"font-family: &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, Tahoma; margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;...(truncated)",

color: rgb(0, 0, 255);\">、</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px; color: rgb(0, 0, 255);\">选专业、毕业等</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px; color: rgb(0, 0, 255);\">造成影响，</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">请同学们结合自身情况慎重申请缓考。</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px; color: rgb(0, 0, 255);\">每门课程只有一次缓考机会，</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">通过缓考申请的同学须在下一学期开学初参加缓考考试，如未参加缓考考试，此门课程按照“</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">0分、旷考”记录成绩单。</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\"><o:p></o:p></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" style=\"text-indent: 24pt; line-height: 21px; margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">&nbsp;5.</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">如学生未申请缓考或缓考申请未通过，擅自不参加考试者，按照“缺考</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\">0分”记录。</span><span style=\"line-height: 24px;\"><o:p></o:p></span></p><p class=\"MsoNormal\" style=\"text-indent: 24pt;
